pointer.clientX;\n151\t      this.publishDrag(swipe, pointer.clientX);\n152\t      return true;\n153\t    }\n154\t    if (verticalDistance >= DRAG_INTENT_SLOP_PX && verticalDistance > Math.abs(horizontalDelta)) {\n155\t      this.activeSwipe = null;\n156\t    }\n157\t    return false;\n158\t  }\n159\t\n160\t  private settle(pointer: Pick<SwipePointer, \"pointerId\">, allowFling: boolean): MobileDrawerSwipeOutcome | null {\n161\t    const swipe = this.activeSwipe;\n162\t    if (!swipe || pointer.pointerId !== swipe.pointerId) {\n163\t      return null;\n164\t    }\n165\t    this.activeSwipe = null;\n166\t    if (!swipe.dragging) {\n167\t      return null;\n168\t    }\n169\t\n170\t    const snapshot = this.snapshot;\n171\t    const progress = snapshot.phase === \"dragging\" ? snapshot.progress : 0;\n172\t    const flingVelocity = allowFling ? swipe.velocityX : 0;\n173\t    let outcome: MobileDrawerSwipeOutcome;\n174\t    if (flingVelocity >= FLING_VELOCITY_PX_PER_MS) {\n175\t      outcome = \"open\";\n176\t    } else if (flingVelocity <= -FLING_VELOCITY_PX_PER_MS) {\n177\t      outcome = \"close\";\n178\t    } else {\n179\t      const travelled = swipe.mode === \"open\" ? progress : 1 - progress;\n180\t      const committed = travelled >= COMMIT_DISTANCE_RATIO;\n181\t      outcome = committed === (swipe.mode === \"open\") ? \"open\" : \"close\";\n182\t    }\n183\t\n184\t    // Opening drops the inline drag transform so the sheet's own transition\n185\t    // carries it the rest of the way in. Closing keeps it pinned off-screen\n186\t    // until the sheet finishes unmounting.\n187\t    this.setSnapshot(outcome === \"open\" ? IDLE_SNAPSHOT : { phase: \"closing\" });\n188\t    return outcome;\n189\t  }\n190\t\n191\t  finish(pointer: SwipePointer) {\n192\t    if (this.activeSwipe?.dragging && pointer.pointerId === this.activeSwipe.pointerId) {\n193\t      this.move(pointer);\n194\t    }\n195\t    return this.settle(pointer, true);\n196\t  }\n197\t\n198\t  cancel(pointer: Pick<SwipePointer, \"pointerId\">) {\n199\t    return this.settle(pointer, false);\n200\t  }\n201\t\n202\t  handleDrawerOpenChangeComplete(open: boolean) {\n203\t    if (!open && this.snapshot.phase === \"closing\") {\n204\t      this.setSnapshot(IDLE_SNAPSHOT);\n205\t    }\n206\t  }\n207\t}\n208\t\n209\texport const mobileConversationSwipeManager = new MobileConversationSwipeManager();"

## Claude thinking · 2026-10-03T21:17:11.868Z

I need to update the unit tests and e2e tests for the new threshold with width 300, giving a 99px cutoff. For the "springs back closed" test I'm adjusting the drag distance to 80px (0.267 ratio) and renaming it to reflect "before a third," while the "opens fully" test moves to 110px (0.367 ratio) renamed "past a third."

Checking the fling and stalled flick tests against the new threshold, they still pass as-is. For the close mode test, I need to change the drag target so the travelled ratio properly triggers open (moving to 200 instead of 140, giving 0.8 progress and 0.2 travelled).

For e2e, I'm recalculating the drawer width at the 390px viewport to get a ~116px threshold, and adjusting the partial-open and full-open drag distances so they correctly validate the new threshold boundary (changing the full open drag to 140px).

## Claude · 2026-10-03T21:18:00.870Z

The drawer now commits at 33% in both directions:

- **Opening:** once a third of the drawer is showing, letting go finishes opening it. Less than that and it slides back closed.
- **Closing:** once you've dragged it a third of the way back, letting go closes it. Less than that and it stays open.

A quick flick still overrides the distance either way.

The change is the `COMMIT_DISTANCE_RATIO = 0.33` constant in `src/interface/home/MobileConversationSwipeManager.ts`. Before, the cut-off was half the drawer width, and it was measured from the closed position for both directions. Now it's measured from wherever the drag started.

The swipe unit tests pass with updated distances, including a 40% drag that now opens and a 37% close that now closes. `pnpm build:interface:web` also succeeds.

I updated the Playwright test for the new threshold but haven't re-run it, because you stopped the earlier run. To check it yourself, run `pnpm exec playwright test tests/e2e/mobile-layout.spec.ts`. Nothing is committed.
